ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>


### [99] [Aray: Deterministic-First Synthesis of Benign Artifacts for YARA Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19387)
*Emanuel C. A. Valente,Lourenço A. P. Júnior,Leonardo Gonçalves Chahud,Júlio Cezar Estrella,Marcus Botacin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Aray，一个确定性优先的YARA解释器与正例夹具合成器，用于为YARA规则自动生成不具恶意行为的匹配测试文件，解决了恶意样本难以分发的问题。在416条公开规则上实现97.6%的整体成功率和100%的可构造规则成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YARA规则易于分发，但用于演示正匹配的恶意样本难以共享，这给存储、持续集成、灾难恢复演练和可复现的扫描器验证带来困难。简单的嵌入字面量无法构造替代夹具，因为YARA条件可以包含替代、计数、偏移、整数读取和可执行容器约束，且生成的文件不应复现恶意行为。

Method: Aray采用确定性优先策略：模型可提出规范化构造或类型化提取回退方案，但绝不接触后端源码或二进制结构。常规代码执行规范化规则验证、字符串和整数见证推导、提取、碰撞检查布局以及ELF/PE/通用序列化，仅残余的规范化语义交给有界模型评审。正例验证是存在性的，只需产生规则匹配集中的一个文件级成员即可。

Result: 在416条公开规则条目上评估：规范化阶段无需模型帮助接受182条，经模型规范化后接受234条；可构造性预检允许406条，所有允许的夹具均与其上游原始规则匹配。整体成功率为406/416（97.6%），可构造规则中为406/406，十个预期预检判定，无扫描器不匹配或构造失败。不可达端点确认实现阶段模型调用为零。

Conclusion: Aray证明通过确定性优先、模型辅助的架构可以高比例地为YARA规则自动生成有效且不具恶意行为的正例测试夹具。原始规则预言机验证生成的夹具匹配源规则，而对所有可能文件证明蕴含关系则是独立的更强目标。两处锚定正则失败已在最终运行前修复，因此结果是修复后系统结果，而非留出法估计。

Abstract: A YARA rule is easy to distribute, but the malware sample used to demonstrate a positive match is not. This complicates storage, continuous integration, disaster-recovery exercises, and reproducible scanner validation. Constructing a replacement fixture requires more than embedding literals: YARA conditions can combine alternatives, counts, offsets, integer reads, and executable-container constraints, while the resulting file should not reproduce malware behavior. Positive validation is existential: it requires one file-level member of a rule's match set, not reconstruction of the originating sample. We present Aray, a deterministic-first YARA interpreter and positive-fixture synthesizer. Models may propose constructive normalizations or typed extraction fallbacks, but never backend source or binary structure. Conventional code validates normalized rules, derives string and integer witnesses, and performs extraction, routing, collision-checked layout, and ELF, PE, or generic serialization. Only residual normalization semantics reach a bounded model judge. We evaluated Aray over 416 public-rule entries. Normalization accepted 182 entries without model assistance and 234 after model normalization. Constructibility preflight admitted 406 entries, and every admitted fixture matched its upstream original rule. This yields 406/416 (97.6%) overall and 406/406 among constructible rules, with ten expected preflight dispositions and no scanner mismatches or construction failures. An unreachable endpoint confirmed zero model invocations during realization. The original-rule oracle validates generated fixtures against their source rules; proving implication for all possible files is a separate, stronger objective. Two anchored-regex failures were repaired before the final run, so these are post-fix systems results, not a held-out estimate.

</details>


### [100] [Redactable blockchains and polynomial equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19401)
*Alexander Demin,Alexey Ovchinnikov,Vladimir Shpilrain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了用于构建可编辑认证数据结构的新工具，其安全性基于后量子假设——具体而言，该方案的单向函数求逆等价于求解多元多项式方程组，在参数选取合理的情况下目前被认为是量子安全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的发展，传统的基于整数分解或离散对数问题的密码学假设面临被量子算法（如Shor算法）攻破的威胁。构建具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构对于保障未来数据完整性验证至关重要。

Method: 提出了一种新的单向函数构造方法，其安全基础在于求解多元多项式方程组（或单个多项式方程）的困难性。该方案通过将求逆问题归约为多元多项式方程求解问题来实现后量子安全性。同时开发了相应的工具来构建可编辑认证数据结构，使得数据所有者可以在不破坏认证性的前提下对数据进行编辑操作。

Result: 该构造在合理参数选择下，被证明在量子计算模型下是安全的，因为目前没有已知的量子算法可以高效求解多元多项式方程组问题。

Conclusion: 基于多元多项式方程求解困难性的可编辑认证数据结构是一种有前景的后量子安全方案，为未来量子时代的数据库与数据验证提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>


### [101] [A Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Oral Cancer Screening on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19462)
*Lena D. Swamikannan,Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Jay S. Patel,C. S. Mani,Lakshmi Narayana,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的联邦学习框架，支持地理分布式AI医疗研究人员协作开发口腔癌筛查模型。框架使用Tailscale实现安全网络连接，基于Flower框架进行服务端聚合，客户端手动部署。在移动端轻量级模型（MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large、MobileNetV4-Conv-Small）中，基于MNv4-Conv-S的全局模型（GM-V4）通过FedAvg聚合后表现最佳，AUC达到0.929，准确率87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域尤其是口腔临床图像的获取受到监管要求和患者隐私的限制，数据难以集中。联邦学习可以在不共享患者数据的条件下实现跨分散数据集的协作建模，因此需要一个实用的FL框架来支持口腔癌筛查模型的开发。

Method: 构建了无需企业级FL平台的实用联邦学习框架：使用Tailscale实现客户端间的安全网络连接和实时通信，采用Flower框架进行服务端聚合，客户端部署和编排手动配置。评估了三种轻量级移动端友好架构（MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large、MobileNetV4-Conv-Small），使用FedAvg进行全局模型聚合。

Result: 在FedAvg聚合的全局轻量级模型中，基于MobileNetV4-Conv-Small的全局模型（GM-V4）性能最优，实现了0.929的AUC和87%的准确率。

Conclusion: 该实用FL框架能够支持跨地理分布的AI医疗研究人员协作，并成功开发出适用于智能手机端口腔癌筛查的轻量级高性能模型，验证了无需企业级平台即可实现有效联邦学习的可行性。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [102] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS，一种轻量级的防御方法，通过三项仅作用于反向传播路径的操作（冻结注意力投影参数、向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声、向MLP扩展梯度注入噪声），同时封堵联邦学习中梯度反转攻击的三个信息泄漏通道，在不改变模型架构且不损害模型效用的情况下将令牌恢复率降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中梯度反转攻击能够从共享的梯度中恢复私有训练文本，严重威胁协作模型训练。现有防御措施最多只针对单一泄漏通道，要么降低模型效用，要么留下其他结构性信号。因此需要一种能够同时关闭所有三个分析通道的轻量级防御方案。

Method: 通过分析Transformer梯度结构识别出三个令牌信息泄漏通道：注意力输出投影梯度中的低秩子空间（通道1）、嵌入梯度行范数稀疏性（通道2）、MLP扩展梯度的可恢复子空间信号（通道3）。AEGIS在反向传播路径上实施三项操作：1) 冻结注意力投影参数从构造上消除通道1；2) 向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声以破坏通道2的令牌存在信号；3) 向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声以掩盖通道3。掩蔽后的梯度同时用于本地优化器更新和服务器导出，确保无干净信号残留。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上的评估表明，AEGIS将令牌恢复率降至接近零，可抵御多种解析型和基于优化的梯度反转攻击，同时保持或改善模型效用。通道1和通道2具有形式化保证，完整防御在自适应对抗者（完全了解机制）场景下也通过了经验验证。

Conclusion: AEGIS提供了一种无需架构修改、轻量且全面的防御方案，同时关闭了Transformer梯度中的三个信息泄漏通道，在保证模型效用的前提下实现近乎完全的隐私保护，并给出了形式化安全保障。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [103] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双重混淆方法，通过联合混淆更新信息和训练图像来增强联邦学习对图像重建攻击的鲁棒性，在ViT图像分类任务上使用APRIL攻击验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能保护数据本地化，但研究表明共享模型更新可被用于重建训练数据，现有防护手段不足以抵御图像恢复攻击。

Method: 该方法由两部分组成：(1) 基于随机二进制权重的鲁棒性增强技术，即将部分梯度元素随机置零以减少攻击者可获取的原始梯度信息；(2) 图像加密技术，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。两种技术互补，且图像加密支持每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥，无需显式密钥共享。

Result: 在ViT图像分类任务上的实验表明，该方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且除图像加密本身造成的退化外，未对分类性能造成额外损失。

Conclusion: 虽然该组合方法不提供绝对安全保证，但实验结果表明梯度修改与图像加密相结合对隐私增强联邦学习具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [104] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT是一个基于信息瓶颈节点引导的APT调查框架，通过图蒸馏、自适应时序图学习和跨时空攻击指纹对齐三个组件，在三个DARPA E3数据集上实现了88.9%-95.7%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS降低约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击具有隐蔽性和多阶段特性，传统检测方法难以有效应对。虽然溯源图提供了丰富的行为上下文，但攻击相关证据稀疏且散布在大量常规系统活动中，全图学习既计算开销大，又难以在长攻击序列中进行关联分析。

Method: TGL-APT基于攻击相关信息非均匀分布且常由结构性影响或行为上独特的实体（信息瓶颈节点）中介的观察，包含三个互补组件：(1)信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏，抑制溯源冗余同时约束结构失真和保持因果可达性；(2)自适应时序图学习，随节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3)跨时空攻击指纹对齐，关联不同实体和时间窗口中的碎片化可疑活动。最后通过因果扩展和阶段特征重构完整的攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上F1分数分别达到95.7%、90.9%和88.9%，相比KAIROS训练时间降低约39%、检测延迟降低约33%、内存使用降低约22%。

Conclusion: TGL-APT有效平衡了检测性能、计算效率和调查能力，验证了信息瓶颈引导图蒸馏在基于溯源图的APT分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [105] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将勒索软件攻击建模为基于搜索的软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，利用遗传算法在统计偏差约束下优化数据加密过程，使攻击模式能够逃避行为监控器的指纹识别，实现长时间潜伏攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件部署依赖大规模加密操作，容易被现代防御系统即时检测。现代威胁中存在"持久性差距"，攻击者希望在数小时而非数分钟内保持隐蔽，因此需要一种新的攻击范式来弥合这一差距。

Method: 将勒索软件执行框架化为SBSE优化问题，采用遗传算法（GA）优化数据加密策略，同时对系统活动与基线的统计偏差设置硬约束，以确保攻击行为在统计上与正常系统活动难以区分。

Result: 实验证明演化出的攻击模式能够规避行为监控器在指纹识别技术下的检测，验证了基于搜索的攻击生成方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 搜索式方法为生成规避性恶意软件提供了强大框架，对自动化软件防御提出了新兴挑战，需要防御方开发更先进的检测机制来应对此类演化型攻击。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [106] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MaliciousSkillBench，一个综合的恶意Agent Skill检测基准，整合13个公开来源、归一化7,539个唯一身份、构建9,740个Skill（7,505恶意+2,235良性）用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills扩展了LLM代理的可复用指令包，可能包含脚本、资源和配置，形成了直接的恶意行为分发渠道。然而现有恶意Skill数据集来源分散、格式不统一、证据体系各异、良性覆盖不足，且重复和结构相关内容使直接聚合与评估变得困难。

Method: 整合13个公开来源，其中11个贡献核心恶意工件；将8,414条原始恶意记录归约为7,539个归一化唯一身份，分布在4,588个操作性结构族中；经保守交叉标签冲突排除后构建包含9,740个Skill的主基准；为4,983个恶意身份协调出11种攻击类别；评估三个学习的文本检测器和三个现成Skill扫描器，包括Random、结构不相交和源不相交三种评估范式。

Result: 学习的检测器在Random评估下达到0.882-0.932的Macro-F1，但在Source-Disjoint评估下仅0.653-0.665；最强的词TF-IDF SVM在Random/结构不相交/Source-Disjoint下得分0.932/0.916/0.665，保留95.6%恶意召回但造成62.4%良性误报率。现成扫描器在不同但不令人满意的运行区间，降低误报以恶意召回大幅下降为代价。

Conclusion: 可靠的恶意Skill检测需要更广泛的跨源基准覆盖，以及同时衡量攻击检测与良性过度标记的联合评估方法。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [107] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个POSIX兼容的文件系统，通过基于密码学承诺和Merkle树的认证数据结构，在可信执行环境（TEE）中为持久存储提供端到端的完整性、新鲜性和防回滚保证，无需修改应用程序，并通过对ZFS的扩展实现（ShieldZFS）获得了与最先进文件系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算（Confidential Computing）仅在TEE内保护计算，但持久存储仍易受恶意云提供商的回滚（rollback）、重放（replay）、分叉（fork）和篡改（tamper）攻击。现有方案要么假定存储可信、要么开销过高、要么将完整性逻辑强加给应用程序，缺乏一个对应用透明且低开销的端到端存储完整性解决方案。

Method: ShieldFS采用以下核心技术：(1) 利用简洁的密码学承诺（succinct cryptographic commitments）表示合法的文件系统状态，承诺保存在TEE内部并复制到轻量级可信注册表中；(2) 磁盘数据结构包括写前日志（WAL）和存储池，通过哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树进行认证；(3) 使用事务和写时复制（copy-on-write）原子地更新持久文件系统状态与承诺；(4) 在读取时验证承诺，确保在I/O栈完全不可信的情况下仍能检测回滚、重放和等分攻击。实现上通过扩展ZFS得到ShieldZFS。

Result: 基于标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载的评估表明，ShieldZFS在提供强完整性（integrity）和新鲜性（freshness）保证的同时，性能与最先进的文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS证明了在不修改应用程序的前提下，可以在机密计算的威胁模型下实现端到端的持久存储完整性保护，且性能开销可接受，为可信云环境中的存储安全提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [108] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath是一种将凭证撤销状态查询移动到持有方的方案，通过零知识证明让持有者证明凭证在验证者选择的注册表根下未被撤销，同时不泄露可观察的元数据。论文首次评估了Verkle树在凭证撤销中的应用，并与稀疏Merkle树进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证凭证的撤销检查会暴露频繁性标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可作为稳定句柄来关联不同的演示（presentations），从而破坏持证者的隐私模型。现有方案无法在不牺牲隐私的情况下完成有效的状态检查。

Method: ShadowPath将凭证状态查询从验证者侧移动到持有者侧。持有者通过Verkle树或稀疏Merkle树结构，以零知识证明方式证明凭证在验证者所选注册表根下未被撤销。采用Groth16证明系统，并引入新鲜的会话随机性以确保会话值独立性。

Result: 30次桌面试验中，Groth16证明的中位耗时：稀疏Merkle为371.6ms，Verkle为2.11s；验证耗时分别为3.70ms和7.55ms。移动设备上Verkle的Groth16证明耗时约3秒。结果表明更短的认证路径并不必然产生更便宜的零知识证明。

Conclusion: ShadowPath能在不泄露可观察元数据的情况下完成撤销检查。研究发现Verkle树缩短路径深度的优势未能抵消基于KZG认证的更高成本。在会话值独立性假设下，验证者可见的状态数据不会揭示两次演示是否使用同一凭证，但该保证排除发行方-验证方共谋和同步流量场景。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [109] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于指令微调LLM的标准化分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（包括边界案例）。在受控网络安全测试平台上生成端到端认证数据，评估了三种指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B、GPT-OSS 20B）与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch统计异常检测的性能。Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct表现最佳（准确率89.3%，F1分数91.8%，FNR 11.8%），并检测出80%的边界异常场景，显著优于Wazuh（准确率52.0%，FNR 68.6%）和OpenSearch（准确率49.3%，FNR 74.5%）。Qwen推理延迟最低且结构化响应有效性100%，GPT-OSS在产生有效响应时分类性能较强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异常日志检测方法（如Wazuh依赖预定义规则、OpenSearch依赖统计偏差）难以识别语义和上下文信息，而LLM-based方法虽具语义理解能力，但受限于提示词构建、噪声日志数据以及缺乏端点特定认证行为的通用数据集。为克服这些限制，需要开发更稳健的检测框架。

Method: 构建标准化的指令式LLM分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（包括边界案例）；在受控网络安全测试平台上生成端点特定的认证数据，构建包含正常、边界和异常行为场景的精选数据集；在统一的地面真值严重性框架下，评估三种指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B），并与Wazuh和OpenSearch进行对比。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct取得最佳端到端检测性能：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、假阴性率11.8%；检测出80%的边界异常场景。Wazuh准确率52.0%、FNR 68.6%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、FNR 74.5%。Qwen检测性能较低但推理延迟最低且100%结构化响应有效性；GPT-OSS在有效响应下分类性能较强。

Conclusion: LLM-based指令分类框架在异常认证行为检测（特别是边界案例）中显著优于传统的规则检测和统计异常检测方法，证明了LLM在网络安全日志分析中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [110] [A Meta-Study on Replication Papers in Usable Security & Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20108)
*Christian Mack,Benjamin Berens,Hanna Algedri,Tobias Hilt,Daniela Reimer,Peter Mayer,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文采用混合方法调查了可用安全与隐私（usable security and privacy）领域中复制研究（replication）的现状，通过分析13个会议/期刊的征稿启事、系统搜索2016-2025年间24篇复制研究论文、使用Olszewski等人提出的复制分类法进行分类，并对作者进行问卷调查，最终提出了四项关键见解和针对学术场所、研究者及审稿人的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用安全与隐私研究是一个年轻且不断扩展的领域，其在研究标准（如复制实践）方面仍在发展中。作者希望深入了解该领域复制研究的当前状态，以推动该领域研究标准的建立和完善。

Method: 采用混合方法：(1) 分析13个涵盖安全、隐私和人机交互领域的会议征稿启事；(2) 系统搜索2016-2025年间发表的复制用户研究论文，筛选出24篇；(3) 使用Olszewski等人（2025）提出的复制分类法对这24篇论文进行分类；(4) 向这些论文的作者分发调查问卷以了解其实施复制的动机。

Result: 四个关键发现：(A) 征稿启事需要为作者和审稿人提供更清晰的复制研究指南；(B) 阅读复制论文时难以确定相对原始研究做了哪些修改；(C) 严格的精确复制（exact replication）在样本中不存在，约三分之二的24项研究修改了原始研究的多个方面；(D) 影响结果的时间性和情境性变化是复制研究最常被引用的动机之一。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，作者为学术场所（venues）、研究者和同行评审者提供了加强可用安全与隐私领域复制实践的实用建议。

Abstract: The field of usable security and privacy research is a young and expanding field, which is still developing standards for its research, e.g. regarding replications. We used a mixed-method approach, in order to get a better understanding of the current state of replications in the field of usable security and privacy: (1) we examine the Call for Papers of 13 venues spanning security, privacy, and human-computer interaction; (2) we conduct a systematic search for papers reporting replicated user studies published across these venues between 2016 and 2025, yielding 24 relevant publications; (3) we categorized these 24 papers employing the replication taxonomy proposed by Olszewski et al. (2025); (4) we distributed a survey to the authors of these papers to understand their motivations for conducting replications. Our analysis reveals four key insights: (A) Calls for Papers would benefit from clearer guidelines for authors and reviewers regarding replication work; (B) determining what modifications were made relative to the original study proves difficult when reading replication papers; (C) strict exact replications do not exist in our sample. Approximately two-thirds of the 24 studies altered multiple aspects of the original work; (D) temporal and contextual changes affecting results emerged as one of the most frequently cited motivations for replication. Based on these findings, we offer practical recommendations for venues, researchers, and peer reviewers to strengthen replication practices in usable security and privacy research.

</details>


### [111] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，使得CellCnn卷积神经网络能够在秘密共享数据上完成训练和推理，在不暴露原始患者数据的前提下，实现与明文版本接近的检测精度，用于识别罕见疾病相关细胞亚群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测罕见疾病相关的细胞亚群（如白血病、病毒感染）对疾病理解至关重要，但CellCnn模型需要大规模多样化患者队列进行可靠训练，而单细胞数据过于敏感，现有隐私法规限制了跨机构的数据共享，因此需要一种既能保障隐私又能保持模型性能的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，将CellCnn的训练和推理过程完全运行在秘密共享数据上。该框架确保参与方和计算服务器均无法观察到原始患者数据或中间值，并且完整保留了CellCnn架构中的ReLU激活函数和偏置项等关键组件（此前的隐私保护方法会移除这些组件）。

Result: 在巨细胞病毒感染（CMV）和急性髓系白血病（AML）两个基准单细胞数据集上评估，所提出的MPC框架在训练和推理精度上接近明文版本的CellCnn，同时优于先前的隐私保护基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在保持完整CellCnn架构的前提下，通过安全多方计算可以实现接近明文水平的检测精度，且不暴露任何原始患者数据，为隐私保护下的跨机构单细胞数据分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values. Evaluated on benchmark single-cell datasets for cytomegalovirus infection (CMV) and acute myeloid leukaemia (AML), our implementation preserves accuracy close to its plaintext counterpart while outperforming the prior privacy-preserving baseline. In contrast to earlier privacy-preserving approaches that removed components such as ReLU activations and bias terms, our method retains these key parts of the CellCnn architecture and supports accurate analysis without exposing raw patient data.

</details>


### [112] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链的信任管理机制，用于解决边缘缓存中的恶意节点识别、自私行为激励和容量限制问题。通过智能合约计算直接与间接信任值，结合奖励机制和认证机制，在考虑移动性、能耗和算力约束下优化信任、缓存容量与成本效率，仿真验证在10%数据噪声下仍能准确区分诚实与恶意服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络流量持续增长，边缘服务器内容缓存对降低延迟至关重要。然而存在三大挑战：恶意边缘服务器可能删除或篡改缓存内容、边缘服务器容量有限、辅助移动节点存在自私行为且可能为恶意节点。需要在容量受限和多源不可信的环境下实现可靠的缓存信任管理。

Method: 提出基于区块链的信任管理机制，包括：(1) 利用智能合约计算直接信任和间接信任，有效过滤恶意节点；(2) 基于移动节点对服务质量满意度来确定信任度，信任数据安全存储于区块链上；(3) 引入奖励机制激励缓存共享以对抗节点自私性；(4) 采用基于区块链的认证机制防止节点冒充；(5) 在共识过程中综合考虑移动节点移动性、能耗和计算能力约束，优化信任、缓存容量和成本效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在数据中存在10%噪声的情况下，所提方法仍能准确区分诚实服务器与恶意服务器。

Conclusion: 基于区块链的信任管理机制能够有效解决边缘缓存中的恶意节点识别、自私节点激励和容量限制问题，通过智能合约实现可信计算与存储，在数据噪声条件下仍保持较高的信任识别准确性。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [113] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Chameleon，一种基于多对多随机流量变形（many-to-many randomized traffic morphing）的鲁棒网站指纹防御方法，通过高类内多样性和低类间差异的候选选择、随机映射以及radix-trie同步机制，在Tor可插拔传输环境中显著降低多种先进WF攻击的有效性，同时控制带宽和时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WF防御大多产生可学习的网页流量映射特征，且对抗训练鲁棒性并不等同于对防御感知自编码器（DAAE）攻击的鲁棒性，需要一种能够打破可学习映射并增强对抗不确定性的新型防御方法。

Method: Chameleon采用多对多随机流量变形：1) 选择具有高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选；2) 将每个网页流量随机映射到多个候选目标，并允许多个网页共享映射目标以增加对抗不确定性；3) 为Tor实际部署设计了基于radix-trie的同步机制，通过包方向前缀让PT端点识别一致变形流量，并引入流量变异和归一化前缀匹配降低开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上、封闭与开放世界设置下，对比六个SOTA防御和五个WF攻击：相比Adaptive Tamaraw，Chameleon将对抗训练攻击准确率降低最多36.74%，同时带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%；在GTT23上的DAAE-RF攻击下将F1限制在35.19%（Adaptive Tamaraw仅为88.22%）；真实PT桥评估中仅增加16.25%时间开销即大幅削弱强WF攻击。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过多对多随机变形打破可学习流量映射特征，在多种攻击和真实部署条件下显著优于现有防御，同时保持较低的开销，为Tor环境下的WF防御提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>


### [114] [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572)
*Mubassir Serneabat Sudipto,Shakil Ahmed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VQC-ZTI，一个用于触觉互联网零信任保护的分裂平面变分量子分类器框架。该框架将异常评分与强制执行分离：离路径VQC分析加密流遥测，在线策略引擎应用缓存的决定性操作（允许、限制、升级、拒绝）。在CESNET衍生流量上，全混合量子神经网络在三种不同划分方式下取得0.9981、0.9974和0.9941的AUC，相比ExtraTrees降低44.6%至67.9%的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网服务将网络事件直接与物理控制耦合，安全决策必须在不干扰控制路径的前提下提升风险判别能力。传统的安全机制难以在低延迟控制场景中同时保证安全性和控制路径的可预测性。

Method: 采用分裂平面（split-plane）架构：离路径（off-path）变分量子分类器（VQC）分析加密流量遥测，在线（on-path）策略引擎应用缓存的确定性操作（grant、restrict、step-up、deny）。通过解耦异常评分与强制执行，使检测器灵敏度与策略激进程度可独立调节。使用混合PyTorch-PennyLane实现，在CESNET衍生流量上采用随机、实体组和时间三种holdouts进行评估。

Result: 全混合量子神经网络在随机、实体组、时间holdouts下分别取得0.9981、0.9974和0.9941的平均AUC；相比ExtraTrees，误报率分别降低44.6%、49.6%和67.9%。组件时间分解表明批量VQC评分处于异步证据路径而非即时强制执行路径。

Conclusion: VQC-ZTI通过离路径量子分类与在线确定性执行的架构设计，在保持触觉互联网控制路径可预测性的同时实现了高精度的零信任安全决策，且量子评分不会引入到关键路径的性能负担。

Abstract: Tactile Internet services couple cyber events directly to physical actuation, so security decisions must improve risk discrimination without perturbing the control path. This paper presents VQC-ZTI, a split-plane Variational Quantum Classifier framework for zero-trust protection of Tactile Internet services, in which an off-path VQC analyzes encrypted-flow telemetry while an on-path policy engine applies cached deterministic grant, restrict, step-up, and deny actions. By decoupling anomaly scoring from enforcement, VQC-ZTI preserves predictable control behavior and allows detector sensitivity and policy aggressiveness to be tuned independently. We evaluate the framework on CESNET-derived aggregated traffic using random, entity-group, and temporal holdouts with a hybrid PyTorch-PennyLane implementation. The full-hybrid Quantum Neural Network achieves mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9981, 0.9974, and 0.9941 and reduces the false-positive rate relative to ExtraTrees by 44.6%, 49.6%, and 67.9%, respectively. A representative component-timing decomposition further illustrates that batched VQC scoring remains in the asynchronous evidence path rather than the immediate enforcement path.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [TorchDCM: A Unified PyTorch-Native Package for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19231)
*Baichuan Mo,Zhengzhong Ricky You,Xiqun Michael Chen,Ruimin Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TorchDCM是一个基于PyTorch的开源Python包，将离散选择模型（DCM）的估计编译为统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎，支持在CPU/CUDA上执行估计、推理、预测和结构化报告。它涵盖了Biogeme和Apollo的主要计量经济功能（多项、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量、面板似然等），相比Biogeme和Apollo可减少89.1%-99.7%的中位运行时间，CUDA额外提供12.0-71.0倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCM软件（如Biogeme、Apollo）提供成熟的计量经济工作流，但基于GPU的工具仅加速特定模型，导致计量经济学覆盖率与可扩展的可微分计算之间存在空白。大规模且仿真密集的DCM估计需要反复评估效用、概率、导数及模拟似然，亟需一个兼顾计量经济学覆盖面与可扩展可微计算的统一工具。

Method: 将选择数据和模型规格编译成统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎，支持CPU和CUDA设备。覆盖多元、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量和面板似然等主要计量经济功能，支持不规则选择集、约束参数、协方差估计、支付意愿分析、弹性计算及可扩展的似然组件。

Result: 与七个其他估计包进行对齐的合成与真实数据全估计实验对比：TorchDCM完成全部45个合成案例，在每个可比较的合成案例中运行最快，且在所有至少有两种可比方案的对比中满足预设的最终对数似然容差。相比Biogeme和Apollo，中位运行时间减少89.1%-99.7%，CUDA比单核TorchDCM额外提供12.0-71.0倍加速。

Conclusion: TorchDCM为计量经济估计和可微分选择模型开发建立了可扩展且可复现的基础，填补了现有DCM软件在可扩展可微计算方面的空白，在性能上大幅超越传统工具。

Abstract: Estimating large and simulation-intensive discrete choice models (DCMs) requires repeated evaluation of utilities, probabilities, derivatives, and simulated likelihoods over many observations, alternatives, and draws. Existing DCM software provides mature econometric workflows, while recent GPU-oriented tools accelerate selected models, leaving a gap between econometric coverage and scalable differentiable computation. We introduce TorchDCM, an open Python package for discrete choice modeling that compiles choice data and model specifications into a unified PyTorch-native likelihood engine for estimation, inference, prediction, and structured reporting on CPU or CUDA devices. The package covers the principal econometric functionality available across Biogeme and Apollo, including multinomial, nested, mixed, ordered, latent-variable, and panel likelihoods. It also supports ragged choice sets, constrained parameters, covariance estimation, willingness-to-pay analysis, elasticities, and extensible likelihood components. We evaluate TorchDCM against seven other estimation packages in aligned synthetic and real-data full-estimation experiments. TorchDCM completes all 45 synthetic cases, runs fastest in every comparable synthetic case, and satisfies the prespecified final-log-likelihood tolerance in every comparison with at least two comparable solutions. More precisely, it reduces median runtime by 89.1%-99.7% relative to Biogeme and Apollo across model-data settings. CUDA provides an additional 12.0-71.0x speedup over single-core TorchDCM. These results establish a scalable and reproducible foundation for econometric estimation and differentiable choice-model development. The open-source package and executed examples are available at https://github.com/mbc96325/torchdcm.

</details>


### [116] [Discrete Diffusion Inference-Time Control with Nested Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20123)
*Lohithsai Yadala Chanchu,Hany Abdulsamad,Christian A. Naesseth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛（NSMC）和全自适应嵌套SMC（FA-NSMC）方法应用于离散扩散语言模型的推理时控制，用于在无需重新训练的情况下将采样引导向序列级奖励，解决现有粒子方法（best-of-n采样和bootstrap SMC）分别存在的过度乐观和权重退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于粒子的方法在离散扩散语言模型的推理时控制中存在缺陷：best-of-n采样容易过度乐观，bootstrap SMC存在权重退化问题，需要更鲁棒的方法来引导采样朝向序列级奖励。

Method: 将嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛（NSMC）和全自适应嵌套SMC（FA-NSMC）方法公式化用于Feynman-Kac引导，识别并修正了先前公式中导致最终估计有偏的错误，并在毒性和流畅性引导任务上进行评估。

Result: NSMC和FA-NSMC在毒性和流畅性引导任务上始终优于best-of-n采样和bootstrap SMC方法。

Conclusion: 嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛方法能够有效克服现有粒子方法在离散扩散语言模型推理时控制中的局限，提供更一致的性能提升。

Abstract: We study inference-time control for text generation in discrete diffusion language models, where the goal is to steer sampling toward sequence-level rewards without retraining. Prior work in this domain has focused on particle-based methods such as best-of-$n$ sampling and bootstrap sequential Monte Carlo, which may suffer from overoptimism and weight degeneracy, respectively. We address these limitations using \emph{nested} sequential Monte Carlo methods. We formulate nested SMC (NSMC) and fully-adapted nested SMC (FA-NSMC) for Feynman--Kac steering, identifying and correcting errors in prior formulations that lead to biased final estimates. We evaluate these methods on toxicity and fluency steering tasks, showing that NSMC and FA-NSMC consistently outperform best-of-$n$ and bootstrap SMC.

</details>


### [117] [Transfer Learning in Nonparametric Regression with Deep ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20255)
*Junpeng Ren,Carlos Misael Madrid Padilla,Yanzhen Chen,Oscar Hernan Madrid Padilla*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于多组数据的非参数回归通用迁移学习框架，采用两阶段偏移学习（offset learning）策略：第一阶段聚合所有组数据估计总体均值函数，第二阶段估计各组的偏移量，最终通过加法组合得到组级估计器。在温和的复杂度和噪声条件下建立了L2误差上界，当使用深度ReLU网络实例化时，在层次组合模型下推导出显式收敛率，展示了克服维数灾难的能力，并通过模拟和真实数据实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组数据的非参数回归中，各组之间往往存在共享结构，如何利用跨组信息进行迁移学习以提升估计精度是一个重要问题。现有方法缺乏一个通用框架来统一处理不同组间共享与差异的结构性关系，且难以在非参数设置下明确刻画迁移带来的收益。

Method: 提出一个两阶段偏移学习框架：（1）第一阶段聚合所有组的数据来估计一个共享的总体均值函数；（2）第二阶段为每组估计其与总体均值的偏移函数，最终通过加法形式组合得到各组的估计器。该框架适用于一类广泛的非参数估计器。当实例化为深度ReLU网络时，在层次组合模型下进行理论分析。

Result: 建立了所提框架在温和条件下（复杂度与噪声条件）的L2误差上界；针对深度ReLU网络，在层次组合模型下推导出显式收敛率，展示了超越维数灾难的能力；分析了能实现正迁移（positive transfer）的条件，包括通过更简单函数学习和通过跨组样本池化进行数据增强；模拟与真实数据实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段偏移学习框架为多组非参数回归提供了通用的迁移学习解决方案，不仅在理论上保证了收敛性，还在深度网络实例下展现了克服维数灾难的可能，并明确了实现正迁移所需的条件，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper develops a general transfer learning framework for nonparametric regression with data consisting of multiple groups. Under the assumption that groups share a common structure along with group-specific deviations in additive form, the proposed method employs a two-stage offset learning procedure: the first stage pools data from all groups to estimate an overall mean function, and the second stage estimates offsets for each group, yielding final group-level estimators through additive combination. Upper bounds on the $\mathcal L_2$ error are established for the proposed framework, covering a broad class of nonparametric estimators under mild complexity and noise conditions. When instantiated with deep ReLU networks, explicit convergence rates are derived under hierarchical composition models, demonstrating the ability to overcome the curse of dimensionality. Conditions that enable positive transfer with faster rates are considered, including learning with simpler functions and data augmentation through pooling samples across groups. Various simulations and real-data experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Tau-Rho Equality and Other Dependence Measures of a Subclass of Factorizable Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19608)
*Noppawit Yanpaisan,Tippawan Santiwipanont,Matthias Scherer,Songkiat Sumetkijakan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究Kendall's tau与Spearman's rho相等的问题，证明所有形如C_{e,α}∗C_{β,e}的因子化copulas满足τ_C=ρ_C，并进一步研究了Chatterjee秩相关系数和尾部相关系数的代数公式与异常现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kendall's tau和Spearman's rho是统计学和风险管理中两个广泛使用的依赖度量，通常被视为可互换的，但Schreyer等人(2017)证明它们可以产生显著分歧。作者希望从互补的角度研究这两个度量何时相等，即寻找满足τ_C=ρ_C的非平凡copula族。

Method: 首先识别因子化copulas C_{e,α}∗C_{β,e}的结构，其中α和β是分段线性单调满射(PLMS)。然后利用代数和解析方法证明此类copulas满足Kendall's tau等于Spearman's rho，并进一步推导Chatterjee秩相关系数和尾部依赖系数的代数公式。

Result: 证明了所有PLMS生成的因子化copulas满足τ_C=ρ_C。对于这类copulas，得到了Chatterjee相关系数和尾部依赖系数的有用代数公式，并发现了意外现象——Chatterjee系数可以表现出极端的非对称性。

Conclusion: 因子化copulas构成了一个满足Kendall's tau与Spearman's rho相等的大族，但其在Chatterjee系数上可能呈现极端的非对称行为，说明不同依赖度量之间即便在特定族内也可能存在显著差异。

Abstract: Kendall's tau and Spearman's rho, two widely used dependence measures in statistics and risk management, are often treated as interchangeable, yet can disagree sharply: Schreyer et al.~(2017) established the exact region of attainable $(τ,ρ)$ pairs. We study the complementary question of equality, namely, identifying nontrivial families of copulas $C$ satisfying $τ_C=ρ_C$. We prove that this equality holds for every factorizable copula of the form $C_{e,α}\ast C_{β,e}$, where $α$ and $β$ are piecewise linear monotonic surjections (PLMS). For these PLMS-generated copulas, we study further dependence measures, including Chatterjee's rank correlation coefficient and tail dependence coefficients, revealing some useful algebraic formulas and unexpected phenomena. In particular, Chatterjee's coefficient can exhibit extreme asymmetry.

</details>


### [119] [Trustworthy Decisions in Reliability Set Estimation under Insufficient Model Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19815)
*Holger Dette,Zhengfu Liu,Jun Yu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将任意工作模型（对真实响应曲面的不完美近似）转化为可信赖的可靠性集合估计决策规则。通过"建模后校准"流程解耦估计与决策，引入非对称可观测的代理损失和校准集来修正阈值偏差，实现O_P(1/n)的体积收敛；同时利用共形风险控制（conformal risk control）以代理损失在预设水平上控制最关键的假包含风险（false inclusion risk）；并通过自适应设计在可靠性集合及其边界集中采样以提升模型质量。数值实验证明该方法比经典plug-in方法具有更准确的集合估计和校准的有限样本风险控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠性集合估计旨在识别响应概率超过目标水平的输入区域，这对于安全关键决策至关重要。然而实际应用中，从业者通常只有不完美的工作模型（对真实响应曲面的近似），直接依赖该模型可能导致将不安全的区域错误地认证为安全区域，从而产生决策风险。现有plug-in方法缺乏有限样本风险控制能力，无法保证决策的可靠性与安全性。

Method: 本文提出一个三步统一的框架：(1) 建模后校准（modeling-then-calibration）程序，通过引入非对称可观测的代理损失，并使用独立的校准集选择偏差校正阈值，从而将估计与决策解耦，实现O_P(1/n)的体积收敛；(2) 利用共形风险控制（conformal risk control）结合代理损失，在任意工作模型质量下将假包含风险（最安全关键的误差）控制于预设水平，并论证了独立校准集对于风险控制的必要性；(3) 自适应试验设计，将采样资源集中投入可靠性集合及其边界区域，以在决策影响最大的位置提升模型质量，同时在其余区域控制预算。

Result: 数值研究表明，所提出的方法相比经典plug-in方法不仅获得更准确的可靠性集合估计，还实现了校准的有限样本风险控制（即假包含风险被控制在预设水平内），而plug-in方法缺乏这种保证。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过对不完善的工作模型进行系统性的阈值校准和共形风险控制，并辅以自适应采样设计，可以在缺乏完美模型的情况下仍获得具有统计保证的、安全可靠的可靠性集合估计，填补了plug-in方法在有限样本风险控制方面的空白。

Abstract: Reliability set estimation identifies input regions where a response probability exceeds a target level, bridging estimation and safety-critical decisions. Practitioners typically start with a working model, an imperfect approximation of the true response surface. Relying on this imperfect model may incur decision risk, potentially certifying unsafe regions as safe. We develop a unified framework that turns such a working model into a trustworthy decision rule. First, a modeling-then-calibration procedure decouples estimation from decision. Since the true set is unobservable, we introduce an asymmetric, observable surrogate loss and use a separate calibration set to select a bias-correcting threshold, reducing decision risk and achieving $O_P(1/n)$ volume convergence. Second, we leverage conformal risk control with the surrogate loss to control false inclusion risk, which is the most safety-critical error, at a pre-specified level regardless of working model quality. Together, these calibration procedures show that a separate calibration set is necessary for risk control. Third, an adaptive design concentrates observations on the reliability set and its boundary, improving model quality where errors most affect decisions while controlling budget elsewhere. Numerical studies show not only more accurate set estimates but also calibrated finite-sample risk control that classical plug-in methods lack.

</details>


### [120] [Combining Concurrent and Historical Functional Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19874)
*Alois Kneip,Dominik Liebl,Sven Otto*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数型-函数型线性回归模型，其中响应变量在时间点t同时取决于预测变量的历史轨迹和其当前值。模型结合了L²历史效应和点评估效应，这两个系数函数并非自动可辨识。作者刻画了由此产生的不可辨识性，并证明当预测变量协方差算子的协方差特征函数不是逐点平方可加时，同期效应和历史效应可分别辨识。基于预测过程的正交化表示，提出平滑样条估计器并建立了收敛速率，发现路径正则性与特征值衰减之间存在权衡。模拟和真实数据应用验证了区分同期效应和历史效应的实际重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在函数型数据分析中，响应变量可能同时受预测变量历史轨迹和历史当前值的影响，但现有模型通常分别处理这两种效应。关键挑战在于这两种效应（L²历史效应和点评估效应）在模型中并非天然可辨识，这阻碍了统计推断和实际应用。

Method: 作者首先从理论上刻画了模型不可辨识性的本质特征，提出了一个新颖的条件（协方差特征函数非逐点平方可加）来确保同期和历史效应的可辨识性。然后基于预测过程的正交化表示，提出了一种平滑样条估计器来同时估计两个系数函数，并推导出其收敛速率。

Result: 建立了估计器的一致性收敛速率，揭示了一个有趣的权衡：预测轨迹越光滑，历史效应估计器收敛越快；而预测轨迹越粗糙，同期效应估计器收敛越快。模拟研究和两个真实数据应用证明了将同期效应与历史效应分离的实际价值。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅在理论上解决了函数型回归中历史效应与同期效应的可辨识性问题，还提供了有效的估计方法，并为实践工作者在选择这类模型时提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a function-on-function linear regression model in which the response at time $t$ depends on both the past trajectory of a predictor and its concurrent value. The model combines an $L^2$-historical effect with a point-evaluation effect, and these two coefficient functions are not automatically identifiable. We characterize the resulting non-identifiability and show that the concurrent and historical effects are separately identifiable whenever the covariance eigenfunctions of the covariance operator of the predictor are not pointwise square-summable. This mild and novel condition prevents the concurrent point evaluation from being represented by an $L^2$-historical effect.
  Building on an orthogonalized representation of the predictor process, we propose a smoothing-spline estimator for both coefficient functions and establish consistency rates. The rates reveal an interesting trade-off between path regularity and eigenvalue decay: smoother predictor trajectories lead to faster convergence of the historical-effect estimator, whereas rougher trajectories lead to faster convergence of the concurrent-effect estimator. Simulation studies and two real-data applications demonstrate the practical importance of disentangling concurrent and historical effects.

</details>


### [121] [The exact Spearman rho-footrule region via optimal transport with applications to finite rankings, mixability, and Chatterjee's rank correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20176)
*Jonathan Ansari,Marcus Rockel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了斯皮尔曼rho在给定斯皮尔曼footrule条件下的最大值这一开放问题，从而完整刻画了这两个量的精确可达区域。作者将问题重构为带线性矩约束的最优运输问题，通过构造匹配的对偶势函数及其接触集得到唯一最优耦合。该结果在有限排序的改进Cauchy-Schwarz不等式、广义可混合性中|U'+V'|的可达常数刻画，以及Chatterjee秩相关与copula相关比的显式界等方面均有应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯皮尔曼rho和斯皮尔曼footrule是两种重要的秩相关度量，此前只解决了部分方向上（给定rho求footrule极值等）的可达区域问题，但在给定footrule时确定rho的最大值这一方向仍是开放问题。完成整个可达区域的刻画对秩相关理论具有基础性意义，并且其结果可推广到其他依赖这两个度量的统计推断框架。

Method: 将copula优化问题转化为带线性矩约束的最优运输问题；通过构造满足互补松弛条件的对偶势函数及其接触集（contact set），证明最优耦合的存在性与唯一性；等价地，在给定E|U-V|均值的所有耦合中最小化Var(|U-V|)。

Result: 完整求解了给定斯皮尔曼footrule时斯皮尔曼rho的最大值，完成了二者的精确可达区域刻画；并由此导出三类应用结果：(1) 有限排序中footrule距离与二次秩差之间的改进Cauchy-Schwarz不等式；(2) 广义可混合性框架下|U'+V'|可达常数值的刻画及给定均值时的最小二次偏差；(3) Chatterjee秩相关xi与copula相关比之间的显式界。

Conclusion: 通过最优运输这一统一数学框架，不仅闭合了斯皮尔曼rho与footrule可达区域的最后缺口，还建立了从秩相关度量到条件i.i.d.表示之间新的理论联系，为有限排序、可混合性及广义相关检测提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: We solve the open problem of determining the maximal value of Spearman's rho when Spearman's footrule is prescribed, thereby completing the exact attainable region of these two quantities. To prove this result, we reformulate the underlying copula optimization problem as an optimal transport problem with a linear moment constraint and construct the unique optimal coupling through a matching feasible dual potential and its contact set. Equivalently, this coupling minimizes the variance of $|U-V|$ among all couplings of $U,V\sim\mathcal{U}(0,1)$ with prescribed mean $\mathbb{E}|U-V|$. Our main result admits several applications: First, in the context of finite rankings, we obtain an improved Cauchy--Schwarz inequality between Spearman's footrule distance and the associated quadratic rank difference. Second, in the framework of generalized mixability, we characterize the attainable constant values of $|U'+V'|$, for $U',V'\sim\mathcal{U}(-1/2,1/2)$, and determine the minimal quadratic deviation for a given mean. Third, we derive explicit bounds relating Chatterjee's rank correlation $ξ(X,Y)$, which can detect complex functional dependence of $Y$ on $X$, to the copula correlation ratio--a rank-based fraction of explained variance--by exploiting their conditional i.i.d. representations in terms of Spearman's footrule and Spearman's rho.

</details>


### [122] [Self-Normalizing Denominators in Rational Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20223)
*Shu Tamano*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究线性结构方程模型中理性因果估计量（协方差多项式之比）的弱识别问题。作者证明在高斯抽样下，某些分母不能在一阶进入弱识别区间，其抽样变异与幅度精确成正比，使得标准化分母在所有样本中恒定。这种性质在任意维度下对嵌套协方差余子式幂的乘积成立，并存在精确的Wishart枢轴量。二维情形的逆命题完全成立；三维情形有一个混合族尚未解决，但因子-秩准则可分类所有含线性或二次无行列式因子的分母，覆盖工具变量、前门和近端公式。对线性前门调整，即使中介残差方差以任意速率消失，只要处理-中介系数非零，Wald推断仍渐近有效。模拟实验中，近端Wald覆盖率随朴素处理-代理诊断指标增强而下降，而前门覆盖率保持名义水平；右心导管数据区分了朴素诊断与分母相关诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱识别是因果推断中的关键问题——当估计量分母很小（接近零）时，标准推断可能失效。已有文献通常将"小分母"直接等同于弱识别，但本文质疑这一假设的普遍性，试图从理论上刻画哪些分母结构真正会引发一阶弱识别问题，哪些分母虽然数值小但抽样变异与其幅度成比例，从而保持推断有效性。

Method: 作者采用理论分析与模拟相结合的方法：(1) 在高斯抽样假设下推导分母的抽样分布性质，证明嵌套协方差余子式幂乘积的标准化分母恒定并具有精确Wishart枢轴量；(2) 在二维空间给出逆命题的完整证明，在三维空间提出因子-秩准则分类分母；(3) 通过模拟实验验证近端和前门公式的Wald推断覆盖表现，并用右心导管数据作为实证案例。

Result: 证明了某些分母（嵌套协方差余子式幂的乘积）的抽样变异与幅度精确成比例，标准化分母恒为常数，因此不会进入一阶弱识别区间。二维情形分类完全；三维情形除一个混合族外，因子-秩准则完整分类了线性/二次无行列式因子。前门调整在中介残差方差趋于零时仍保持Wald推断有效性，而近端推断的诊断指标会误导性预示弱识别。

Conclusion: 弱识别的判定不能仅依据分母大小，而需关注分母的结构性质。本文为区分"真正的弱识别"与"无害的小分母"提供了理论准则，并指出在实证中应使用分母相关的诊断指标而非朴素诊断。

Abstract: Rational causal estimators in linear structural equation models take the form of one covariance polynomial divided by another, and a small denominator is commonly interpreted as weak identification. We show that, under Gaussian sampling, some denominators cannot enter this regime at first order. Their sampling variation is exactly proportional to their magnitude, so the standardized denominator is constant in every sample. Products of powers of nested covariance minors have this property in every dimension and admit an exact Wishart pivot. The converse is complete in dimension two. In dimension three, one mixed family remains open, while a factor-and-rank criterion classifies all denominators with linear or quadratic determinant-free factors and covers instrumental-variable, front-door and proximal formulas. For linear front-door adjustment, Wald inference remains asymptotically valid even as the mediator residual variance vanishes at an arbitrary rate, provided the treatment--mediator coefficient is nonzero. In simulations, proximal Wald coverage fell as a naive treatment--proxy diagnostic strengthened, while front-door coverage stayed nominal, and right-heart-catheterization data distinguished naive from denominator-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [123] [Large Sample Properties of Higher Order Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20321)
*Tuhin Majumder,Donald E. K. Martin,Soumendra N. Lahiri*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究有限字母表上高阶马尔可夫链的大样本性质，允许阶数 m_n 随序列长度 n 增长。通过将过程嵌入 Σ^{m_n} 上的一阶链并利用返回时间分解，在自然遍历性与稀疏性条件下，建立了加性泛函的中心极限定理，并推广到 m_n→∞、m_n/n→0 的三角阵列情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶马尔可夫链（含变量长度马尔可夫链 VLMC 和稀疏马尔可夫模型 SMM）在语言建模、生物序列、压缩与统计推断等场景广泛应用；但当阶数随样本量增长时，传统固定阶数的极限理论不再适用，缺乏相应的大样本理论基础。本文旨在填补这一空白，为有效维度随样本量增长的稀疏/分区高阶模型提供渐近推断基础。

Method: 将 m_n 阶过程嵌入字母表 Σ^{m_n} 上的一阶马尔可夫链，利用返回时间（return time）分解建立正态近似；在遍历性与稀疏性条件下分析三角阵列，归一化采用适选状态的平稳返回时间。并以二元 VLMC 为例，推导平稳质量的显式下界，建立 m_n log m_n / n → 0 的具体增长机制以保证 CLT 成立。

Result: 在自然遍历性和稀疏性条件下，建立了加性泛函的中心极限定理；针对二元 VLMC 给出了 m_n log m_n / n → 0 的具体增长条件，验证了理论假设的可满足性。

Conclusion: 所得结果为阶数随样本量增长的高阶马尔可夫模型（包括 VLMC 和 SMM）提供了渐近推断的理论基础，扩展了经典固定阶数马尔可夫链极限理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We study large-sample properties of higher-order Markov chains on a finite alphabet $Σ$ when the order $m_n$ is allowed to grow with the sequence length $n$. By embedding the process into a first-order chain on $Σ^{m_n}$ and exploiting return-time decompositions, we establish a central limit theorem for additive functionals $\sum_{t}\! g_n(Y_t^{(n)})$ under natural ergodicity and sparsity conditions. The normalization involves the stationary return time to a suitably chosen state and accommodates triangular arrays with $m_n\!\to\!\infty$ and $m_n/n\!\to\!0$. We further illustrate the assumptions in a binary variable length Markov chain (VLMC), deriving explicit lower bounds on stationary masses that yield a concrete growth regime (e.g., $m_n\log m_n/n \to 0$) ensuring the CLT. These results provide asymptotic foundations for inference in sparse/partitioned higher-order models; including VLMCs and sparse Markov models (SMMs) where the effective dimensionality grows with the sample size.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LSTM循环神经网络的监督式机器学习模型，用于估算直升机起飞重量。该研究基于空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集，并遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准建立了学习保证流程。研究定义了机器学习需求、模型描述及实现方案，并在传统航电计算机上验证了需求的满足性，展示了该模型在机载关键功能（如机上告警）上的部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是影响飞行安全和性能的关键参数，传统估算方法可能不够精确或实时性不足。航空领域对机载系统有严格的适航要求，因此需要一种既有高精度又符合航空安全认证标准的机器学习方法来实现直升机起飞重量的准确、实时估算，同时解决ML模型在航空航天关键功能中应用所面临的安全认证与部署挑战。

Method: 论文提出了一套完整的学习保证流程：首先定义了机器学习需求（ML Requirements）和机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description）；随后采用LSTM（长短期记忆）循环神经网络实现重量估算模型；该模型使用空客全球在役机队的大量数据集进行监督式训练。最后，在传统航电计算机（legacy avionics computers）上对实现进行了需求验证，确认其满足设定要求。

Result: 研究成功构建并验证了一个基于LSTM的直升机起飞重量估算模型。该模型在传统航电计算平台上完成了需求验证，证明了其满足EASA概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准框架下的机器学习需求。实现方案适用于在机载环境中部署，可支持诸如机上告警等关键功能。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将监督式LSTM机器学习模型应用于直升机起飞重量估算的可行性与合规性。通过遵循EASA和Eurocae的安全认证框架，该实现方案能够部署于传统航空电子设备上，服务于关键飞行功能，为机器学习模型在航空航天机载系统中的应用提供了实践经验与参考路径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [125] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三角模糊数重缩放距离(d_TR)，将线性重缩放直接集成到距离计算中，解决异构属性（不同尺度/单位）下模糊数比较时需预先归一化的问题。作者正式证明了该度量满足度量空间的全部性质（非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式），且具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三角模糊数距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，当面对不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需要进行预归一化处理，这增加了复杂性和潜在的误差。作者希望提出一种将归一化内置于距离计算过程的度量方法。

Method: 提出Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_TR)度量，其核心创新在于将线性重缩放(LRE)直接集成到三角模糊数的距离计算过程中，无需单独的预处理阶段。作者从理论上形式化证明了该距离满足度量的四个基本性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并进一步证明了其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，同时引入了权重向量以支持维度优先级的设定。

Result: 形式化证明了d_TR是严格意义上的度量（满足全部度量公理），并具备有界性、尺度不变性、原点不变性等关键性质，这些特性使其天然适用于异构模糊数据的场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的d_TR距离度量解决了模糊距离计算中异构数据需预归一化的问题，结合权重向量机制，为合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等应用提供了具有理论保证的工具。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [126] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态心电图数据集（含22,980个QA对，来源于788份临床Holter记录，并创新性地引入信号-视频-文本三模态对齐），并基于此构建Holtercare-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结上的能力。零样本评估显示现有MLLM在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距，但微调后性能大幅提升，为长期医学MLLM提供了基础性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在医学应用中大多偏向静态图像或短期信号，而在动态心电图（ECG）这一关键领域，模型缺乏复杂时间推理和诊断报告生成能力，根本原因在于缺乏高质量的数据集和基准。

Method: 1) 构建Holtercare-23K数据集，包含22,980个QA对和788份临床Holter记录，创新性地提出信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；2) 基于该数据集构建Holtercare-Bench多模态基准，评估时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度；3) 对代表性MLLM进行零样本评估和微调实验。

Result: 零样本评估显示主流MLLM在处理超长病理序列方面存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后，性能获得大幅提升。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了当前MLLM在电生理学领域的局限性，并为长期医学MLLM提供了基础性基准和高质量数据集，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子-经典混合机器学习在基于cfDNA（游离DNA）片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用，使用角度和密集角度特征映射将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间，并与经典SVM基线进行性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT肺癌筛查虽能降低死亡率，但受限于摄取率、依从性和管理挑战。cfDNA生物标志物提供补充方案，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测仍然困难，需要更强大的机器学习方法来捕捉这些复杂的非线性模式。

Method: 使用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化特征，经特征选择后采用20和40特征子集。特征通过角度和密集角度特征映射编码到量子希尔伯特空间，结合多种纠缠策略；基于保真度的量子核采用精确态矢量模拟计算，并与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，与基于原始特征训练的SVM基线进行比较。

Result: 跨多次留出评估中，量子核模型在两个数据集上均达到有竞争力的性能。片段组学方面，多个20特征配置相比经典SVM基线提升了AUC；甲基化方面，经典SVM实现最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力且在特异性上有所提升。特征数从20增至40并未持续改善性能，反而常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的有前景途径得到支持，其编码和纠缠设计系统评估框架为量子机器学习在生物医学诊断中的应用提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [128] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用标注数据集来增强LLM不确定性量化（UQ）的方法：通过使用现有零样本不确定性分数以及相似查询的正确性作为特征，构建简单的分类器来预测LLM响应的正确性，从而在保持极低计算开销的同时一致性地超越现有的UQ分数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化方法（如口头化置信度、多次生成等）多为零样本方法，无需标注数据即可输出不确定性分数；但在实际部署前，总需要在特定数据集上评估其性能。作者希望利用这一评估过程（即使用该数据集）来进一步提升UQ表现。

Method: 构建简单的分类器，以现有UQ分数和相似查询（similar queries）的正确性作为特征，预测LLM响应的正确性。该方法基于数据集进行训练/校准，分类器结构简单，计算开销极小。

Result: 在利用目标数据集后，该方法一致性地优于现有的零样本UQ分数方法，同时仅引入极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过在部署前利用数据集构建简单分类器，可以为LLM的不确定性量化提供一种低成本、易实施的增强方案，适用于真实世界应用场景。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [129] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积和子集干预等多种机制可解释性测量统一为y=Ax+w的共享测量结构，并给出按成本递增的实用测量程序。在控制任务、前向访问、梯度访问、多模型（Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI、Qwen-2.5-7B）上验证该方法，证明其能恢复有限效应图、局部归因图及跨组交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性的目标是获取模型未直接暴露的量（表示状态、组件效应、交互、干预响应），但现有方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积、子集干预）在访问假设和测量目标上各不相同，缺乏统一的测量框架来指导实践者选择测量方案并解释结果。

Method: 提出机制断层扫描框架：在选定基和干预族下，测量形式为y=Ax+w。给出实用流程：从最低成本测量开始，在保留的干预上以目标规模测试，校准简单失配，若存在结构化残差则扩展测量族。在控制设置（两HMM模型）、前向访问、梯度访问、Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI和Qwen-2.5-7B上验证。

Result: 控制任务中控制误差随观测误差上升；前向访问下稀疏聚合测量比坐标式patching用更少干预恢复有限效应图；梯度访问下有限探针改进局部归因图；提升测量和Hessian-向量积可恢复一阶图未捕捉的交互；GPT-2-small IOI中Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是最大的保留预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上加性拒绝响应图经有限校准已足够，不需要成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描提供了一个统一的测量语言和实用的逐级测量流程，强调"按需测量"——从简单开始，仅在结构化残差出现时才扩展测量族。不同模型所需的测量族依赖基的选择，证实测量复杂度的选择应与具体基准和访问假设匹配。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [130] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板加速的神经网络鲁棒性验证技术，提出新颖的"联合稳定神经元"指标解释模板归并率的变化规律，并设计FastCert技术自动跨层分配模板以提升验证性能，在L0验证任务上平均获得1.13倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板复用在特定场景下可以加速不完整验证技术，但其跨不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法的鲁棒性尚不明确，需要系统性研究其有效性与局限性。

Method: 首先进行系统性实验研究模板加速的效果边界，提出联合稳定神经元（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板归并率的变化；在此基础上设计FastCert，该技术自动在神经网络的各层间分配模板，并在模板不太可能产生加速时完全放弃使用模板。

Result: 在大量基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上，FastCert相比现有模板复用技术平均获得1.13倍的加速；研究同时表明模板归并率在不同场景下差异很大，某些情况下模板技术几乎不可能带来任何加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速并非普适有效，其效果高度依赖具体场景；联合稳定神经元指标可预测其适用性，FastCert通过自适应模板分配策略能够在收益不确定时仍然提供稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [131] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)框架，一个纵向贝叶斯学习模型，通过整合纵向扩散张量成像（DTI）数据与弱临床监督，估计阿尔茨海默病（AD）的疾病严重程度，并导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)以连续量化个体在AD疾病连续体中的位置及不确定性。在ADNI队列上的实验表明，DCP优于现有疾病进展方法，DCS能准确刻画疾病严重程度、具备临床相关性、保持纵向演化规律并预测未来疾病转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病本质上是一个连续的生物过程，但现有基于神经影像的人工智能方法大多局限于从横断面影像中进行离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续进展特性，缺乏纵向建模能力。

Method: 提出DCP框架：（1）将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量；（2）联合整合纵向观测数据与弱临床监督进行贝叶斯学习；（3）从潜变量导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)，用于量化个体在AD疾病连续体中的位置并给出不确定性估计。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明：DCP在疾病进展方法比较中持续优于代表性基线；验证分析显示DCS准确刻画疾病严重程度、具有强临床相关性、保持纵向疾病演化特征，并能预测未来疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了超越传统诊断标签和临床评分的定量影像学表征，可用于AD进展的连续评估，弥补了现有方法在疾病连续过程建模方面的不足。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [132] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime，一个多分辨率框架，用于预测网络安全事件披露后的短期异常损失。它结合了长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据，并引入动态对比学习目标以缓解稀疏监督问题。作者还构建了SECURE数据集（网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列和语义特征对齐），实验证明其优于现有SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件等外生冲击会突然破坏金融时间序列并导致显著异常损失。现有时间序列预测模型主要聚焦于趋势、季节性、自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件。此外，高影响事件稀疏且背景市场噪声大，进一步加剧了挑战。作者旨在解决这一事件条件化的冲击影响预测问题。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，融合三部分信息：(1)长期市场背景、(2)短期事件前动态、(3)事件元数据。核心组件包括一个事件融合模块，将时间序列表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关近期市场模式；以及一个动态对比学习目标，在训练过程中构建事件感知和时间序列感知的正负样本对，以缓解稀疏监督问题。同时构建了SECURE真实数据集。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估算事件后财务损失方面持续优于最先进的时序预测和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其具有更强的事件敏感表示、对不完整元数据更具鲁棒性，以及对网络安全披露后的短期市场影响估计更具可解释性。

Conclusion: EventTime有效解决了外生网络事件对金融时间序列冲击的预测问题，通过多分辨率信息融合和对比学习策略克服了稀疏事件监督和噪声干扰的挑战，为事件驱动型金融风险预测提供了新的框架和方法。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses.
